They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.111 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.091 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.911 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.111 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.091 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.911 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 11.912 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = -0.667 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 6.400 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 11.912 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = -0.667 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 6.400 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.245 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.067 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 5.333 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.245 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.067 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 5.333 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.314 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.087 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.511 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.314 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.087 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.511 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.294 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.069 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.511 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.294 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.069 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.511 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

They wanted to know if the results confirm the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>You helped them set up the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>, so you know that part is correct, but they got confused and were unsure about how to calculate the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>. So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.022 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.845 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.644 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.022 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.845 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.644 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.067 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.042 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.133 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.067 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.042 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.133 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.103 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.089 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 2.244 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.103 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.089 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 2.244 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = -0.244 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.888 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.644 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = -0.244 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.888 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 1.644 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct

So much so that they did it three (3) different ways.</p> <p>Before you ask your instructor for a new lab partner, <b>tell them which table is correct AND whether they can reject or fail to reject the <span style='color: #0247fe;'>null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span></b> using the information provided.</p>	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.083 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.138 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.244 is correct and we <span style="color: #e60000;"><strong>REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #5d3b00;"><strong>Table 1</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 0.083 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #004d99;"><strong>Table 2</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.138 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Incorrect	<span style="color: #b30077;"><strong>Table 3</strong></span> with &chi;&sup2; = 3.244 is correct and we <span style="color: #009900;"><strong>FAIL to REJECT</strong></span> the <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span>	Correct
